905 entries, ledger-to-journal-writeup to legacy-code-testing. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ledger-to-journal-writeup**: Convert mathematical proof ledgers, historical route notes, exploratory drafts, or campaign archives into journal-style self-contained LaTeX writeups. Use when Codex needs to clean a proof ledger into a polished theorem/proof note, remove chronology and campaign jargon, consolidate current results or chains of one-use lemmas, add definitions/examples/reading guides/dependency maps, audit proof contracts for gaps, extract a proof-only companion note from campaign history, or decide which results should be promoted to theorem-level statements. ([obousquet/obousquet.github.io](https://github.com/obousquet/obousquet.github.io/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ledger-to-journal-writeup))
- **ledger-to-notion**: Read payment screenshots, receipt photos/PDFs, KakaoTalk/SMS payment notices, or card/bank export files and add the transactions as rows to the user's Notion household-ledger (가계부) database, after showing a preview and getting approval. Also clones a ledger template when the user has none, adapts to an existing ledger, and sets up an optional daily Slack-DM reminder routine. Use when the user wants to log spending, says "put this in my 가계부", or sends Naver Pay / Kakao Pay / card / Toss payment images. ([K2YOUNG/ledger-to-notion-plugin](https://github.com/K2YOUNG/ledger-to-notion-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/ledger-to-notion))
- **ledger-tooling-testing**: CLI end-to-end verification of the episode-ledger tools (tools/ledger.nim, tools/highlight_cuts.nim, tools/report_card.nim) — how to get ledgers, how to build an independent oracle to check derived beats/stats, and which edge cases actually distinguish right from wrong. ([softmax-universe/coworld-battle-royale](https://github.com/softmax-universe/coworld-battle-royale/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/ledger-tooling-testing) · ★1)
